Status precedence: CONFLICTING_VERSION = differing substantive same-path versions across refs; DUPLICATE = identical bytes also located elsewhere; UNVERIFIED = located but withheld or result attestation absent; FOUND = located source; MISSING = no dedicated category artifact. These flags do not certify requirements approval. Shared multi-module FSD is stored once in Cross-Module HMS; module-specific MISSING cells mean no dedicated file, not that the shared FSD has no relevant section.

## Cleanup interpretation

Design coverage cells reflect retained `.md`/`.txt` copies after removing 91 unsupported-format copies. A Design MISSING cell can therefore coexist with a historical source-discovery count: that original artifact still exists but is excluded from the consolidated Design format policy. Other category cells and original ref snapshots are unchanged. Full removed-source provenance is retained in the manifest history.
